    }

    if (kind === 'voice') {

      micSpeaking = false;

      micVadTimer = null;
      closePipeline(micPipeline);
      micPipeline = null;

  async function startScreenShare(sourceInput) {
    if (!roomState.connected) throw new Error('Entre em uma sala antes de compartilhar a tela.');
    /* ESTUDEX_V193_HOTFIX14_SOURCE_METADATA_V2 */
    const sourceMeta=sourceInput&&typeof sourceInput==='object'?sourceInput:null;

  async function handleMessage(message){
    const type=String(message?.type||'');if(!callTypes.has(type))return false;

  restartRoomDevicesIfNeeded=async function exactApplyAllV1010RoomPreferences(){
    const qualityMap={'720p':720,'1080p':1080,'1440p':1440};
    const quality=qualityMap[clean(roomPrefs.quality)]||1080;
    const rawFps=Number(roomPrefs.fps);
    const fps=[24,30,60].includes(rawFps)?rawFps:60;
    await E.media.setMicrophoneDevice(roomPrefs.micDeviceId==='default'?'':roomPrefs.micDeviceId);
    await E.media.setCameraDevice(roomPrefs.cameraDeviceId==='default'?'':roomPrefs.cameraDeviceId);

  function renderPicker(){

  async function pickCaptureSource(){
    const sources=await E.media.listCaptureSources();
    pickerSources=(Array.isArray(sources)?sources:[]).filter(item=>clean(item?.id));
    if(!pickerSources.length)throw new Error('Nenhuma tela ou janela disponível para compartilhar.');

    pickerSelected=clean(preferred?.id);
    const layer=ensurePicker();
    renderPicker();
    layer.classList.remove('hidden');
    return new Promise(resolve=>{pickerResolve=resolve;});
  }

  async function handleControl(event){
    const b=event.target.closest('[data-call]');if(!b)return;const action=b.dataset.call,state=E.calls.getState();
    try{if(action==='mic')await E.calls.setMicrophoneEnabled(!state.microphoneEnabled);else if(action==='audio')E.calls.setAudioEnabled(!state.audioEnabled);else if(action==='camera')await E.calls.setCameraEnabled(!state.cameraEnabled);else if(action==='screen'){if(state.screenShareEnabled)await E.calls.stopScreenShare();else await E.calls.startScreenShare();}else if(action==='fullscreen'){const stage=q('#specCallStage');if(document.fullscreenElement)await document.exitFullscreen();else await stage?.requestFullscreen?.();}else if(action==='end')await E.calls.end();}catch(error){showToast?.(error?.message||'Não foi possível alterar a chamada.');}
  }

  async function start(mode){const active=thread();if(!active){showToast?.('Selecione uma conversa primeiro.');return;}try{const current=E.calls.getState();if(current.status!=='idle'&&current.peerId===active.username){if(mode==='video')await E.calls.setCameraEnabled(true);else if(mode==='screen')await E.calls.startScreenShare();return;}await E.calls.start(active.username,mode);showPage?.('inbox');}catch(error){showToast?.(error?.message||'Usuário indisponível.');}}

  function hsvToHex(h,s,v){const c=v*s,x=c*(1-Math.abs((h/60)%2-1)),m=v-c;let r=0,g=0,b=0;if(h<60)[r,g,b]=[c,x,0];else if(h<120)[r,g,b]=[x,c,0];else if(h<180)[r,g,b]=[0,c,x];else if(h<240)[r,g,b]=[0,x,c];else if(h<300)[r,g,b]=[x,0,c];else[r,g,b]=[c,0,x];return'#'+[r,g,b].map(n=>Math.round((n+m)*255).toString(16).padStart(2,'0')).join('').toUpperCase();}
  function hexToHsv(hex){const v=normalizeHex(hex)||'#2563EB',r=parseInt(v.slice(1,3),16)/255,g=parseInt(v.slice(3,5),16)/255,b=parseInt(v.slice(5,7),16)/255,max=Math.max(r,g,b),min=Math.min(r,g,b),d=max-min;let h=0;if(d){if(max===r)h=60*(((g-b)/d)%6);else if(max===g)h=60*((b-r)/d+2);else h=60*((r-g)/d+4);}if(h<0)h+=360;return{h,s:max?d/max:0,v:max};}
  function installColorPicker(){
    const card=q('.color-picker-card');if(!card||q('#specColorPicker',card))return false;

    let hsv=hexToHsv(localStorage.getItem('estudex-theme-color')||getComputedStyle(document.documentElement).getPropertyValue('--theme-color')||'#2563EB');
    const render=()=>{const hex=hsvToHex(hsv.h,hsv.s,hsv.v);picker.style.setProperty('--spec-hue',String(hsv.h));picker.style.setProperty('--spec-color',hex);q('#specHue').value=String(Math.round(hsv.h));q('#specPickerHex').value=hex;q('#specSvDot').style.left=(hsv.s*100)+'%';q('#specSvDot').style.top=((1-hsv.v)*100)+'%';};

function bindPointerDrag(element, handler) {
  let dragging = false;

  element.addEventListener("pointerdown", event => {
    dragging = true;
>>>     element.setPointerCapture(event.pointerId);
    handler(event);
  });

  element.addEventListener("pointermove", event => {
    if (dragging) {
      handler(event);
    }
  });

  element.addEventListener("pointerup", event => {
    dragging = false;

    try {
      element.releasePointerCapture(event.pointerId);
    } catch {}
  });

    const haystack = `${room.title || ""} ${room.subtitle || ""} ${room.code || ""}`.toLowerCase();
    if (haystack.includes(q)) add({type:"room", key:room.code, name:room.title || "Sala", subtitle:`Sala recente · #${room.code || ""}`, code:room.code});
  });

  (socialFriends || []).forEach(friend => {
    const haystack = `${friend.name || ""} ${friend.username || ""}`.toLowerCase();
    if (haystack.includes(q)) add({type:"friend", key:friend.id, name:friend.name || friend.username, subtitle:friendStatusLabel(friend), avatar:friend.avatar || null, friendId:friend.id});
  });

  Object.values(inboxThreads || {}).forEach(thread => {
    const haystack = `${thread.name || ""} ${thread.username || ""}`.toLowerCase();
    if (haystack.includes(q)) add({type:"conversation", key:thread.peerKey, name:thread.name || thread.username, subtitle:"Conversa na Caixa de entrada", avatar:thread.avatar || null, peerKey:thread.peerKey});
  });

  return results.slice(0, 16);
}

00213:   async function chooseCaptureSource() {

00215:     const list = Array.isArray(sources) ? sources.filter(item => clean(item?.id)) : [];

00219:     return list.find(item => item?.isPrimary) || list.find(item => /screen/i.test(clean(item?.id))) || list[0];
